    ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    L1 · System context — the machine manager
    One double-bordered manager box; providers (BM = bare metal) and machines stay outside it, S3 and Slack beyond. Two arrows run between manager and machines, one each way.

                                   ╭───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╮
                                   │                                                                       │
                                   │                                                                       │
                                   │                            ╭──────────────╮                           │
                    iPXE, heartbeat│          ╭────────────────▶│providers (BM)│──────────────────╮        │
                                   │          │ provider API    │              │ allocate instance│        │
                                   ▼          │                 ╰──────────────╯                  │        │
                           ╔═══════════════╗  │                                                   │        │
                           ║               ║●─╯                ╭───────────────╮                  │        ●
    ╭──────────────╮http   ║               ║    diagnose     ╭▶│S3 object store│                  │ ╭─────────────╮
    │humans + mmctl│●────╮ ║               ║─────────────────╯ │               │                  ╰▶│             │
    │              │     ╰▶║machine manager║                   ╰───────────────╯                    │machines (BM)│
    ╰──────────────╯       ║               ║●───────────────╮                                     ╭▶│             │
                           ║               ║                │       ╭─────╮                       │ ╰─────────────╯
                           ║               ║────────────────⌒──────▶│Slack│                       │
                           ╚═══════════════╝                │       │     │                       │
                                          confirm + alerts  │       ╰─────╯                       │
                                                            │                                     │
                                                            │             boot, audit             │
                                                            ╰─────────────────────────────────────╯


    ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    L2 · Containers — request, provisioning and boot
    Dotted box = the manager's own containers; the caller, providers (BM) and machines stay outside it. Actions become a River job, a provider allocation, then a boot.

                           ···································································································································

                           ·                                                   reinstall/reprovision        ╭───────────╮ provision job ╭▶│provider registry│·─────────────╮
                           ·                                           ╭───────────────────────────────────▶│           │───────────────╯ │                 │·provider API │ ╭──────────────╮

     │              │      ·                                              │            │───────────────╯                                       │        │    ·           │                                       │machines (BM)│                  │boot server│

                           ·╰─────────────╯                                                            │  │reconcile loop│              │ │ │  ╰────────╯    ·           │  ╰───────────────╯

    ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    L2 · Containers — heartbeat, audits and diagnostics
    Dotted box = the manager's own containers; machines (BM), S3 and Slack stay outside it. Agent heartbeat, audit and diagnose jobs, janitor proposals and Slack alerts.

     │S3 object store│    ·╰─────────────╯ ╰───────────────────│River queue│───────────────────────▶│janitor app│─────────────────╯ ╰─────────────╯     │confirm + al·rts  │

    L1 · System context — the machine manager

    ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    L1 · System context — the machine manager
    One double-bordered manager box; providers (BM = bare metal) and machines stay outside it, S3 and Slack beyond. Two arrows run between manager and machines, one each way.

                                   ╭─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────●─╮

    ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    L1 · System context — the machine manager

    L1 · System context — the machine manager
    One double-bordered manager box; providers (BM = bare metal) and machines stay outside it, S3 and Slack beyond. Two arrows run between manager and machines, one each way.

    ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    L1 · System context — the machine manager
    One double-bordered manager box; providers (BM = bare metal) and machines stay outside it, S3 and Slack beyond. Two arrows run between manager and machines, one each way.

    ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    L1 · System context — the machine manager
    One double-bordered manager box; providers (BM = bare metal) and machines stay outside it, S3 and Slack beyond. Two arrows run between manager and machines, one each way.

                                   ╭──────────────────────────────────────────────────●────────────────────╮
                                   │                                                                       │
                                   │                                                                       │

    │humans + mmctl│───●─╮ ║               ║─────────────────╯ │               │                  ╰▶│             │
    │              │     ╰▶║machine manager║                   ╰───────────────╯                    │machines (BM)│

    ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    L2 · Containers — request, provisioning and boot
    Dotted box = the manager's own containers; the caller, providers (BM) and machines stay outside it. Actions become a River job, a provider allocation, then a boot.
